*SEGPUR
BGN
To indicate the beginning of a transaction set
*SEGPUR
BIN
To transfer binary data in a single data segment and allow identification of 
the end of the data segment through a count; there is no identification of 
the internal structure of the binary data in this segment
*SEGPUR
CAT
To specify categories of patient information service
*SEGPUR
DTP
To specify any or all of a date, a time, or a time period
*SEGPUR
EFI
To provide basic information about the electronic format of the interchange 
data
*SEGPUR
LX
To reference a line number in a transaction set
*SEGPUR
N3
To specify the location of the named party
*SEGPUR
N4
To specify the geographic place of the named party
*SEGPUR
NM1
To supply the full name of an individual or organizational entity
*SEGPUR
NX1
To define the attributes of a property or an entity
*SEGPUR
PER
To identify a person or office to whom administrative communications should 
be directed
*SEGPUR
PRV
To specify the identifying characteristics of a provider
*SEGPUR
REF
To specify identifying information
*SEGPUR
SE
To indicate the end of the transaction set and provide the count of the 
transmitted segments (including the beginning (ST) and ending (SE) segments)
*SEGPUR
ST
To indicate the start of a transaction set and to assign a control number
*SEGPUR
STC
To report the status, required action, and paid information of a claim or 
service line
*SEGPUR
TRN
To uniquely identify a transaction to an application

*SEGNTE
SE,00,C,1

*SEGNTE
ST,01,S,1
The transaction set identifier (ST01) is used by the translation routines of 
the interchange partners to select the appropriate transaction set 
definition (e.g., 810 selects the Invoice Transaction Set).
*SEGNTE
ST,03,S,1
The implementation convention reference (ST03) is used by the translation 
routines of the interchange partners to select the appropriate 
implementation convention to match the transaction set definition. When 
used, this implementation convention reference takes precedence over the 
implementation reference specified in the GS08.
*SEGNTE
STC,02,S,1

*SEGNTE
STC,04,S,1

*SEGNTE
STC,05,S,1
STC05 is the amount paid.
*SEGNTE
STC,06,S,1
STC06 is the paid date.
*SEGNTE
STC,08,S,1
STC08 is the check issue date.
*SEGNTE
STC,12,S,1
STC12 allows additional free-form status information.

*SEGNTE

*COMPUR
C035
To provide provider specialty information
*COMPUR
C040
To identify one or more reference numbers or identification numbers as 
specified by the Reference Qualifier
*COMPUR
C043
Used to convey status of the entire claim or a specific service line
*COMNTE
C035,02,S,1
C035-02 qualifies C035-01.
*COMNTE
C035,03,S,1
C035-03 is the provider specialty certification indicator. A "Y" value 
indicates the provider is certified in the specialty indicated. An "N" value 
indicates the provider is not certified in the specialty indicated. A "U" 
value indicates that it is unknown whether the provider is certified in the 
specialty indicated.
*COMNTE
C040,03,N,1
P0304
*COMNTE
C040,05,N,1
P0506
*COMNTE
C043,01,S,1
C043-01 is used to specify the logical groupings of Health Care Claim Status 
Codes (See Code Source 507).
*COMNTE
C043,02,S,1
C043-02 is used to identify the status of an entire claim or a serviceline. 
Code Source 508 is referenced unless qualified by C043-04.
*COMNTE
C043,03,S,1
C043-03 identifies the entity associated with the Health Care Claim Status 
Code.
*COMNTE
C043,04,S,1
C043-04 is used to identify the Code Source referenced in C043-02.

337
Time expressed in 24-hour clock time as follows: HHMM, or HHMMSS, or 
HHMMSSD, or HHMMSSDD, where H = hours (00-23), M = minutes (00-59), S = 
integer seconds (00-59) and DD = decimal seconds; decimal seconds are 
expressed as follows: D = tenths (0-9) and DD = hundredths (00-99)

Code identifying the time. In accordance with International Standards 
Organization standard 8601, time can be specified by a + or - and an 
indication in hours in relation to Universal Time Coordinate (UTC) time;

codes that follow
